## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route: an agenda that returns as a marker (slides 5, 11, 28), numbered priorities 1 to 8 on slide 4 that recur as red tags and icons on later slides, and a closing slide 31 that restates the RoTE target. Consistent chart styling, red-for-HSBC colour logic and action-style titles make most pages readable as claim plus evidence, and the pairing of data with short claims on slides 7, 8 and 18 works well. The weaknesses are density and some unclear charts: slides 13 and 29 carry visuals that do not communicate on their own (a bubble chart with no readable y or x scale values on slide 13, a waterfall with no values on most steps on slide 29), and many pages (20, 22, 25, 27) are tiny-text tables that a room audience could not read. The legal slide 2 is a full wall of fine print and the final slide 32 is a blank grey page.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the numbered priority tags and icons that tie slides 4, 12 and 13 to 27, the agenda slides that mark section changes, the action titles, and the red-for-HSBC highlighting against grey peers on slides 7, 10 and 21.

**Most useful repair:** Label the waterfall steps on slide 29 with values, add readable axes or scale cues to the bubble chart on slide 13, and cut the footnote-sized text on slides 20, 22 and 25 so the headline numbers stand out.

**What would change the score:** Split the densest slides (4, 12, 20, 27) into a headline page plus backup, and tighten the summary on slide 3 so the bolded phrases carry the message.

_Confidence medium. 32 of 32 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is HSBC's June 2018 strategy update for investors. It opens with a title slide and a formal notice covering forward-looking statements and non-GAAP measures, then gives a one-page summary of the strategy. The deck moves through four main sections: a profile of the bank as a leading international bank with access to high-growth markets and a strong balance sheet; a review of the transformation since 2011 and the plan for growth in Asia, the US, the UK and transaction banking; the financial framework covering costs, investment of USD15-17bn to 2020, capital and dividends; and a closing conclusion. It closes by restating the target of a return on tangible equity above 11% by 2020 with positive adjusted jaws. Slides worth studying include the strategy summary grid, the market-share charts that pair data with short claims, the wealth-in-Asia breakdown, and the capital bridge that separates the stated target from the underlying drivers. The deck uses dense tables, numbered priority codes that recur across slides, and consistent before-and-after charts to link each initiative to a measurable outcome.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the HSBC Strategy Update investor presentation from June 2018 have?**

The deck has 32 pages in total, and the title slide is slide 1. The page count is listed in the deck's own metadata rather than on a single slide.

**What return on tangible equity target does the HSBC strategy update set out?**

Slide 3 states that the Group targets a RoTE of >11% by 2020 while delivering positive adjusted jaws on an annual basis and sustaining its dividend. Slide 4 adds a footnote that a targeted reported RoTE of 11% in 2020 is broadly equivalent to a reported return on equity of 10%, assuming a Group CET1 ratio greater than 14%.

**How much does HSBC plan to invest until 2020 according to its strategy deck?**

Slide 3 says the Group is investing USD15-17bn until 2020, primarily in growth and technology, while delivering positive adjusted jaws. Slide 3 also describes the next phase of strategy as returning the Group to growth and improving returns.

**How is the HSBC strategy update deck structured?**

The agenda on slide 5 lists three sections: leading international bank with platform for growth and signature balance sheet strength, next phase of strategy focused on return to growth and value creation, and profitable growth to deliver RoTE above 11% by 2020. The agenda is repeated on slide 11, and the deck includes an important notice and forward-looking statements on slide 2.

**Layout**

Single full-bleed photograph with left-aligned title block in the upper-left area: title at about 8.9% from the top, subtitle directly beneath it, both starting at 3.0% from the left edge. The HSBC logo sits in the bottom-left corner at about 4% margin. No footer, no slide number, no page furniture beyond the logo.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed black-and-white photograph of a historic Hong Kong harbourfront: grey hills at left and right, colonial stone buildings along the waterfront, a calm grey harbour in the foreground, and an overcast pale grey sky. Overlaid in the centre is a large stylised diamond composed of triangular facets. Inside the facets is a full-colour night view of the skyline in warm orange and red tones, with lit towers, a glowing red-lit tower at centre, and a illuminated ferris wheel at right. The overlay is cropped by the diamond shape and is not boxed. Lighting is night-time glow inside the facets against daylight monochrome outside. Dominant colours are grey, charcoal, red #C8102E-range and warm amber. Logo: HSBC hexagon mark, a red #DB0011 hexagon with white stylised cross-like inner shape, next to black HSBC wordmark, in bottom-left corner.

**Design**

Background: Black-and-white and colour-tinted photograph of Hong Kong harbour over a #FFFFFF base fill, covering the full slide. Light grey and white photographic background with a #FFFFFF base fill. Title in Arial regular, red #DB0011, sentence case, about 4.4% of slide height for the title size. Subtitle in Arial regular, black #000000, about 3.0% of slide height. Text is flush left with no shapes, lines, borders, shadows or gradients. Photograph is the sole graphic treatment.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Split the densest slides (4, 12, 20, 27) into a headline page plus backup, and tighten the summary on slide 3 so the bolded phrases carry the message.

**Text**

HSBC Strategy Update
Summary of the strategy
Leading international bank with platform for growth and signature balance sheet strength
World’s leading international bank and No 1 global transaction bank
Unparalleled access to high growth markets and coverage of trade corridors between them
Recognised for signature balance sheet strength – foundation for future growth and a stable dividend
Next phase of our strategy is to return the Group to growth, improve returns, and enhance customer and employee experience
After a period of restructuring, supported by normalising interest rates and synchronised economic growth, it is time for HSBC to get back into growth mode
Accelerate growth in areas of strength with higher capital efficiency, in particular in Asia and from our international network
Leverage our size and strength to embrace new technologies over a period of disruptive technological change. Investing USD15-17bn until 2020 primarily in growth and technology while delivering positive adjusted jaws
Complete the turnaround in the US
Simplify the organisation and invest in capabilities for the future
As a result of these strategic priorities, the Group targets a RoTE of >11% by 2020 while delivering positive adjusted jaws on an annual basis and sustaining our dividend
3

**Layout**

Single-column text slide on a white field. A small italic running header 'HSBC Strategy Update' sits top-left above a full-width black rule at about 5.7% height, and the red title 'Summary of the strategy' is left-aligned at 5.6% from the left under it. All body content sits inside one thin red rectangular frame spanning about 5.7% to 94.5% horizontally and 13.6% to 89.7% vertically, with text inset from its left edge at about 7.7% to 9.5%. A thin red rule runs just below the frame at about 90.7% height, and the slide number '3' sits bottom-right at about 96.9% from the left and 95.4% from the top.

**Visuals**

none (no photographs, charts, icons or logos; the only graphic elements are the thin red rectangle outline framing the body text, the black header rule and the thin red rule below the frame, and the red square bullet glyphs).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate text slide. Background white #FFFFFF. Title 'Summary of the strategy' is regular-weight red #DB0011 sans-serif (Arial), about 4.0% of slide height (cap height about 2.8%). Running header is italic Arial in black #000000 at about 2.1% of slide height. Section headings are bold Arial red #DB0011 at about 2.8% of slide height. Body and bullet text is Arial at about 2.8% of slide height, mixing regular and bold in black #000000, with bold used for key phrases. Bullets are red Wingdings square glyphs #DB0011. Frame is a red #DB0011 outline rectangle of about 1 px stroke with square corners and no shadow or fill. Header rule is black, about 1 px. Lower red rule is #DB0011, about 1 px. Page number is Arial in #252525 at about 1.7% of slide height. No gradients or shadows.

### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A single grid maps six strategic priorities to financial targets and numbered codes that recur on later slides, so the audience can follow each theme through the deck.
- Review: The deck has a clear route: an agenda that returns as a marker (slides 5, 11, 28), numbered priorities 1 to 8 on slide 4 that recur as red tags and icons on later slides, and a closing slide 31 that restates the RoTE target.
- Worth keeping: Keep the numbered priority tags and icons that tie slides 4, 12 and 13 to 27, the agenda slides that mark section changes, the action titles, and the red-for-HSBC highlighting against grey peers on slides 7, 10 and 21.

**Text**

HSBC Strategy Update / Strategic priorities to deliver growth, improve returns, and enhance / customer and employee experience / Strategic priorities / Financial targets / 1 / Accelerate growth from our Asian franchise / Build on strength in Hong Kong / Invest in PRD, ASEAN, and Wealth in Asia (incl. / Insurance and Asset Management) / Be the leading bank to support drivers of global / investment: China-led Belt and Road Initiative and the / transition to a low carbon economy / Deliver growth / from areas of / strength / 2 / Complete establishment of UK ring-fenced bank, / increase mortgage market share, grow commercial / customer base, and improve customer service / 3 / Gain market share and deliver growth from our / international network / Turnaround of / low-return / businesses / 4 / Turn around our US business / 5 / Improve capital efficiency; redeploy capital into higher / return businesses / Build a bank for / the future that / puts the / customer at the / centre / 6 / Create capacity for increasing investments in growth / and technology through efficiency gains / 7 / Enhance customer centricity and customer service / through investments in technology / Invest in digital capabilities to deliver improved / customer service / Expand the reach of HSBC, including partnerships / Safeguard our customers and deliver industry- / leading financial crime standards / Empower our / people / 8 / Simplify the organisation and invest in future skills / Financial targets / RoTE1 / >11% by 2020 / Costs / Positive jaws / (adjusted, on an / annual basis) / Capital / and / dividend / Sustain dividends / through long-term / earnings capacity / of the businesses / Share buy-backs / subject to / regulatory / approval / 4 / 1. Return on tangible equity (‘RoTE’) is calculated as reported profit attributable to ordinary shareholders less changes in goodwill and present value of in-force long term insurance business divided by average tangible shareholders’ / equity. A targeted reported RoTE of 11% in 2020 is broadly equivalent to a reported return on equity (‘RoE’) of 10%; assumes a Group CET1 ratio greater than 14%

**Layout**

Single white slide with a two-line red title at top left under an italic header and a black rule, aligned to a left margin of about 5.6% of width. The body is two boxed columns: a wide left panel holding the numbered strategic priorities with left-side theme icons and labels, and a narrower right panel titled Financial targets with three grey label blocks. A red footer rule sits near the bottom with footnote text beneath it and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four black icons in the left panel, aligned to the left column. (1) Bar chart icon: four ascending black bars with a black arrow rising diagonally across them, on a light grey hatched square, next to the label Deliver growth from areas of strength. (2) Circular icon: a black circle containing a white dollar sign, with two black curved arrows circling around it, next to the label Turnaround of low-return businesses. (3) Group icon: three black silhouette figures of people, one large in front and two smaller behind, next to the label Build a bank for the future that puts the customer at the centre. (4) Figure icon: a black silhouette of a person with arms raised and legs apart in a jumping pose, next to the label Empower our people. Eight solid red squares with white numerals 1 to 8 mark the priorities. Three light grey rectangles sit in the right panel behind the Financial targets labels RoTE1, Costs and Capital and dividend. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the full slide. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or shadows. Title in red #DB0011 Arial regular, about 4.0% of slide height per line, line spacing about 1.1. Body text in black #000000 Arial regular, about 2.0% of slide height, with bold Arial for priority headings and key phrases. Section labels and headings are red #DB0011 Arial bold or bold italic at about 2.0%. Header is black #000000 Arial italic about 2.1%. Panels are thin red #DB0011 outlines of about 0.75pt with square corners and no fill. Dividing rules are light grey #A6A6A6 hairlines about 0.5pt. Number squares are solid red #DB0011 with white bold numerals, square corners. Grey label blocks are #D7D8D6 with no border. Bullets are small red squares #FF0000 in Wingdings. Footer rule is red #DB0011 about 1pt. Footnote text is black Arial about 1.2% of slide height.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear route: an agenda that returns as a marker (slides 5, 11, 28), numbered priorities 1 to 8 on slide 4 that recur as red tags and icons on later slides, and a closing slide 31 that restates the RoTE target.

**Text**

HSBC Strategy Update
Agenda
1
Leading international bank with platform for growth and / signature balance sheet strength
2
Next phase of strategy: Return to growth and value creation
3
Profitable growth to deliver RoTE > 11% by 2020
5

**Layout**

Single-column agenda on a white slide. A small italic running header sits at top left above a full-width black rule, with the red title Agenda left-aligned below it at the left margin (about 5.6% from left). Three full-width horizontal rows are stacked in the middle of the slide, each with a narrow numeral box at left (about 11% to 18% x) and a wide text bar to its right (about 20.2% to 90.2% x), with equal gaps between rows. A thin red rule runs across the bottom above the page number, which sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three-row agenda graphic built from flat rectangles, no photographs or icons. Row 1 is a red box with white bold numeral 1 beside a red bar holding white bold two-line text. Rows 2 and 3 are light grey boxes with black regular numerals and black regular single-line text. Thin black horizontal rule under the header and thin red horizontal rule near the bottom, both spanning the content width.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title Agenda in red #DB0011, sans-serif (Arial regular), about 4.0% of slide height. Header text HSBC Strategy Update in black #000000 Arial italic about 2.1% of slide height. Body text in row bars is Arial about 3.15% of slide height: bold white #FFFFFF on the active row, regular black #000000 on the grey rows. Accent red #DB0011. Grey fills #D7D8D6. Shapes are square-cornered flat rectangles with no shadows or gradients. Rules are thin, black under header and red at bottom.

### Slide 6

**Text**

Leading international bank with platform for growth and signature balance sheet strength / Leading international bank with a platform for growth and signature / balance sheet strength / Who we are / Strategic differentiators / c.38m / customers served / by 229k / colleagues¹ / 67 markets / Covering >90% / of global GDP, / trade and capital / flows / #1 / global transaction / bank² / #1 / International bank / in Asia³ / USD182bn / Total capital⁴ / Top 3 / FTSE dividend / payer⁵ / 1 Leading international bank / >50% of Group client revenue connected to the network / No 1 global transaction bank, gaining market share / Recognised by customers as leading international bank / 2 Unparalleled access to high growth markets / Access to high growth developing markets in Asia, / Middle East and Latin America / Investment aligned to high growth markets to deliver / shareholder value / 3 Signature balance sheet strength / Strong capital, funding and liquidity position with / diversified business model / Conservative approach to credit risk and liquidity / management / Low earnings volatility / Strong capital position and intrinsic capital generation / Foundation for sustained dividend; strong capacity for / distribution to shareholders / 1. Full-time equivalent as at 31 Dec 2017 / 2. Based on 2017 Transaction Banking product total revenue (including Payments, Cash Management, Trade / Finance, FX and Securities Services) compared with US and European peers. Source: HSBC Research / 3. Based on 2017 total revenue in Asia among major international and regional banks in Asia. Peers include / Standard Chartered, DBS, Citi, UOB, OCBC, Maybank and CIMB. Source: Company accounts / 4. As at 31 Dec 2017 / 5. Total USD payout (2015-2017) / 6

**Layout**

Two columns below a title block: a left column of six equal grey tiles in a 2-by-3 grid under the heading 'Who we are' and a right column of three numbered differentiator groups under 'Strategic differentiators', each heading with a red rule beneath. The title sits top-left below a small italic running header and a black rule; footnotes sit in two columns above a full-width red rule near the bottom, with the page number at bottom right; left margin about 5.6% and right margin about 5.6%.

**Visuals**

None apart from the six flat grey stat tiles (#E6E6E6, no border, no shadow, square corners), three red square number markers (1, 2, 3) with white numerals, and red square bullet markers (Wingdings ▪) in #DB0011 for the list items; no photographs, charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with flat, unshadowed shapes; titles and headings in red #DB0011; body text and footnotes in black #000000; accents in red #DB0011 (headings, rules, markers, stat figures); page number in #252525. Typefaces are Arial (sans-serif) regular for the title, body and footnotes, Arial Bold for headings, stat labels and numerals, and Arial Italic for the running header. Title text is about 4.0% of slide height, headings about 2.5%, body about 2.5%, footnotes about 1.2%; large stat figures about 4.2%. Red rules are thin lines under the section headings and above the footer; a black thin rule sits under the running header.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Review: Consistent chart styling, red-for-HSBC colour logic and action-style titles make most pages readable as claim plus evidence, and the pairing of data with short claims on slides 7, 8 and 18 works well.
- Worth keeping: Keep the numbered priority tags and icons that tie slides 4, 12 and 13 to 27, the agenda slides that mark section changes, the action titles, and the red-for-HSBC highlighting against grey peers on slides 7, 10 and 21.

**Text**

Leading international bank with platform for growth and signature balance sheet strength / 1 Leading international bank with high return transaction banking / Leading transaction banking franchises¹ / FY2017, revenue, USDbn / HSBC 15.2 / US Bank / US Bank / US Bank / European Bank / European Bank / European Bank / European Bank / European Bank / Recognised as leading international bank / % of large corporates choosing HSBC as their lead international bank² / 26% / 22% / 21% / Asia / US / Europe / RoTE Transaction Banking, % / >20% / 20% / 9% / HSBC Transaction Banking / Transaction Banking Industry¹ / Banking Industry overall⁷ / Leading market positions / #1 / bank for Trade Finance³ / #1 / bank for FX for corporates⁴ / #1 / bank for Liquidity and account management³ / #1 / For Assets Under Custody in Asia Pacific⁵ / #2 / bank for Emerging Markets Fixed Income⁶ / 53% / of client revenue connected to international network / 1. Revenue from GTRF, GLCM, FX and Securities Services, compared with peer equivalents. Source: Company financial data; HSBC adjusted revenue / 2. Greenwich Associates – Large Corporate Banking / 3. Oliver Wyman / 4. Greenwich Survey; G10 + EM countries / 5. EY, based on data provided by HSBC and Tricumen / 6. EM Macro; McKinsey/ Coalition / 7. McKinsey / 7

**Layout**

Three columns under a single-line red title: a left column (bar chart, about x 5.6 to 37 percent), a centre column (two column charts, x 36.7 to 64.5 percent), and a right column (a 2 by 3 grid of grey tiles, x 68.6 to 94.5 percent). Column headings sit at y 17 to 25 percent, each with a red rule beneath. The red title sits at top left after a red numbered box; a black rule with an italic tagline sits above it. Footnotes occupy two columns at the bottom above a red rule, with the slide number at bottom right (x 96.9, y 95.4).

**Visuals**

Three charts and six tiles. Left: horizontal bar chart titled 'Leading transaction banking franchises', subtitle FY2017, revenue, USDbn. Bars are ranked, largest at top: HSBC (red #DB0011) labelled 15.2; then US Bank, US Bank, US Bank, and five European Bank bars, all grey #B4B4B4. Only the HSBC value is printed. Other bars are about 14.3, 13.3, 10.3, 5.3, 5.0, 4.4, 4.3 and 2.1 USDbn, estimated from bar length. No axis scale is printed; a thin black vertical axis line runs at the bar origin. Middle top: column chart titled 'Recognised as leading international bank', subtitle '% of large corporates choosing HSBC as their lead international bank', columns in red #DB0011: Asia 26%, US 22%, Europe 21%, with a black horizontal baseline. Middle bottom: column chart titled 'RoTE Transaction Banking, %': HSBC Transaction Banking >20% (red), Transaction Banking Industry 20% (grey #B4B4B4), Banking Industry overall 9% (grey #B4B4B4); black baseline. Right: six square-cornered tiles in light grey #D7D8D6, each with a red or black figure and bold black text: #1 bank for Trade Finance; #1 bank for FX for corporates; #1 bank for Liquidity and account management; #1 For Assets Under Custody in Asia Pacific; #2 bank for Emerging Markets Fixed Income; 53% of client revenue connected to international network. The 53% figure and #1 and #2 figures are red #DB0011. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Titles and section headings are red #DB0011 bold sans-serif (Arial Bold). Body and chart labels are black #000000 regular Arial. Tagline is black #000000 Arial Italic. Accent colour is red #DB0011; neutral grey #B4B4B4 for bars and #D7D8D6 for tiles. Title text is about 4.0 percent of slide height; headings and body about 2.1 percent; footnotes about 1.2 percent. Rules are thin: a black rule under the tagline and red rules under column headings and above the footnotes. Bars and tiles are flat with no corner radius, shadows or gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: Consistent chart styling, red-for-HSBC colour logic and action-style titles make most pages readable as claim plus evidence, and the pairing of data with short claims on slides 7, 8 and 18 works well.

Engagement in Saudi Arabia primarily through investment in Saudi British Bank (SABB); held as an Associate of HSBC / 8. By assets in 2017 from MENA regional bank financials / 9. Euromoney Trade Finance Survey 2018 and Euromoney Cash Management Survey 2017 / 10. Best International Bank for BRI in 2017 Asiamoney New Silk Road Finance Awards / 11. National Commission of Banking and Securities (Mexico) / 8

**Layout**

Two columns divided by a thin vertical red rule at about x 49: the left column (x 5.6 to 37) stacks two bar-chart panels, GDP growth above world trade growth, each with a red heading and red underline; the right column (x 41 to 94) stacks market-share blocks for Asia, Middle East and Latin America, each with a red heading and red underline. A black italic running header sits at top left with a full-width black rule beneath it, the red numbered title follows at top left, and two columns of footnotes sit above a red rule at the bottom, with the page number 8 at bottom right. Left margin about 5.6% and right margin about 5.8% of slide width.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, World Nominal GDP growth 2017-2030 (stacked bar chart, upper left): two stacked columns labelled 2017 and 2030E on a shared black baseline; no numeric axis or segment values printed; 2030E column about twice the height of 2017 column (about 2.1 times). Stack segments from top: Asia red #DB0011, Middle East salmon pink, Africa dark grey, Latin America charcoal, N. America light grey, Europe very light grey. A black diagonal arrow with an arrowhead points up-right from lower left to upper right behind a white ellipse with black outline labelled +5.8%. Legend to the right of the bars lists Asia, Middle East, Africa, Latin America, N. America, Europe with matching colour squares. A CAGR column headed CAGR (underlined) lists values in white ellipses with black outlines: Asia 7.4% and Middle East 7.4% on pink-filled ellipses (highlighted), Africa 6.5%, Latin America 6.0%, N. America 4.3%, Europe 4.6%. Chart 2, World Trade Growth 2017-2030 (stacked bar chart, lower left): same structure with columns 2017 and 2030E, black diagonal arrow labelled +6.1%, legend Asia, Middle East, Africa, Latin America, N. America, Europe; CAGR column Asia 7.1% (pink-filled, highlighted), Middle East 7.8% (pink-filled, highlighted), Africa 8.0%, Latin America 5.7%, N. America 4.4%, Europe 5.4%. Right side: red bold percentage figures with bullet squares (red Wingdings square bullets) and no icons, logos or photographs. Market-share figures: Hong Kong 29%, PRD 12%, Singapore 5%, Malaysia 3%, Saudi Arabia 8%, United Arab Emirates 4%, Mexico 8%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and section headings in red #DB0011 bold sans-serif; body text black #000000 in Arial regular with bold emphasis words in Arial Bold. Running header in Arial Italic black. Title is Arial regular in red, about 4.0% of slide height; section headings about 2.1% of slide height bold red; body text about 2.1% of slide height; footnotes about 1.2% of slide height. Red accent rules are thin solid #DB0011 lines about 0.2% of slide height; black header rule thin. Ellipses have 1 px black outlines and white fill, with pink #FFC0C0 fill for highlighted CAGR values. Bars have no outlines and no shadows; no rounded corners beyond the ellipses.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Leading international bank with platform for growth and signature balance sheet strength / Access to domestic growth in eight markets; network to connect / trade and capital flows / 2 / Aspiration / Characteristics / Markets / % of adj. / revenue / FY17 / Markets at / scale / “HSBC is / considered one of / the leading / domestic banks” / Top 5 bank, at least 3-5% / market share / At least USD1bn revenue / Universal bank / Full participation across / customer segments / Hong Kong / UK / Mexico / PRD / Singapore / Malaysia / UAE / Saudi / Arabia1 / c.60% / Markets as / leading / international / bank / “HSBC is the / leading international / bank in the country” / Leading international bank / At least USD0.5bn revenue / Wholesale bank or Universal / bank with very focused retail / offering (where strategic) / Australia / Canada / China / France / Germany / India / Indonesia / US / c.25% / Markets to / connect the / network / “HSBC is in the / country to connect / foreign and local / customers to our / network” / Targeted offering for / international customers / Wholesale-focused / Branch or rep office where / possible / Network markets to / connect trade and capital / flows (e.g. Japan, Spain, / Brazil) / Supporting subsidiaries of / global customers / c.15% / 1. / Engagement in Saudi Arabia primarily through investment in Saudi British Bank (SABB); held as an Associate of HSBC / 9

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a small italic running header at the top, a red numbered title below it, and a four-column table (row labels, Aspiration, Characteristics, Markets, plus a narrow % of adj. revenue column) with three stacked rows separated by thin grey rules. A thin red rule closes the content above a footnote at the bottom left, and the page number sits at the bottom right. Left margin about 5.6%, right margin about 5.6%. Column headers are red bold and sit above red underlines; the title sits top-left with the red number box at its left.

**Visuals**

Three parchment-style quote cards, one per row, each about 20.5% wide by 18.4% high, with torn, deckled edges and a soft drop shadow, cream to tan colour (about #F3DFC0 with darker edges). Row 1 card reads “HSBC is considered one of the leading domestic banks”; row 2 reads “HSBC is the leading international bank in the country”; row 3 reads “HSBC is in the country to connect foreign and local customers to our network”. Three grey (#B4B4B4) rectangular row-label boxes at left, each about 11% by 17.8%, with bold black labels. Three ovals outlining percentages in the % of adj. revenue column: c.60% (row 1), c.25% (row 2), c.15% (row 3), each a thin black outline about 5.9% wide by 5% high with bold black text, unfilled. Red square with white bold number 2 at top left of the title. Red square bullets (Wingdings) in all bullet lists. No photographs, logos or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

Two-column comparison on a white slide. The left column (x 5.6 to 49.2) holds a 'Strong balance sheet' table with three grey category blocks and three rows each, and the right column (x 50.7 to 94.5) holds a 'Low-risk model' bar comparison with six grey label blocks and paired HSBC and peer bars. The headline sits top-left below a small italic running header and a black rule, with a red numbered square '3' before it; the slide has a red rule above the footnotes and the page number bottom-right at x 96.2, y 95.4.

**Visuals**

Two bar-style comparison panels and one tabular panel; no photographs or logos. Left panel is a table: three grey category blocks (Balance Sheet; Capital; Funding and liquidity) with values in light grey boxes: Customer accounts 1.4tn; Loans & advances to customers 1.0tn; Total equity 198bn; Total regulatory capital 182bn; Leverage ratio 5.6%; Total capital ratio 20.9%; Advances to deposits ratio 71%; Liquidity coverage ratio 142%; Liquid asset buffer >500bn. Grey separator lines sit between Total equity and Total regulatory capital, and between Total capital ratio and Advances to deposits ratio. Right panel is a bar chart with two series, HSBC (red bars, #DB0011) and Peer group average (grey bars, #B4B4B4), each bar with its value printed above it and a black baseline beneath each row. Rows, HSBC value then peer value: 10 year PBT volatility² 1.0x / 2.6x (HSBC bar about one-third of peer bar height); LICs / loans and advances³ 0.2% / 0.9%; Advances to deposits ratio 71% / 82%; Leverage ratio 5.6% / 5.5%⁴; Total capital ratio 20.9% / 17.1%; CET1 ratio 14.5% / 13.0%. No axes or legend are drawn; bar heights are proportional to the printed values within each row only (values about, where not printed: none).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Headline in Arial regular red #DB0011 at about 4.0% of slide height. Section headings in Arial bold red #DB0011 at about 2.1% of slide height. Table labels and values in Arial bold black #000000 at about 2.1%; bar values in Arial regular black #000000 at about 2.1%. Running header in Arial italic black #000000 at about 2.1%. Footnotes and source in Arial regular black #000000 at about 1.2%. Category blocks and peer bars in grey #B4B4B4 with no border; value boxes in light grey #D9D9D9; HSBC bars and the red number square in red #DB0011. Rules: black 1pt line under the running header; red 1pt underlines beneath section headings and column headings; red 1pt footer rule; grey 0.75pt separators; black 0.75pt baselines under bars. All shapes have square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear route: an agenda that returns as a marker (slides 5, 11, 28), numbered priorities 1 to 8 on slide 4 that recur as red tags and icons on later slides, and a closing slide 31 that restates the RoTE target.

**Text**

HSBC Strategy Update / Agenda / 1 / Leading international bank with platform for growth and signature / balance sheet strength / 2 / Next phase of strategy: Return to growth and value creation / 3 / Profitable growth to deliver RoTE > 11% by 2020 / 11

**Layout**

Single-column agenda with a running header at top left, a red title below it, and a three-row list of numbered bars centred in the middle of the slide. A thin black rule runs under the header and a thin red rule sits near the bottom; the page number is at the bottom right. Left margin about 11% and right edge about 90%.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title 'Agenda' in red #DB0011, regular Arial-style sans-serif, about 4.0% of slide height. Header 'HSBC Strategy Update' in italic Arial-style sans-serif, about 2.1% of slide height, black #000000. Body text in Arial-style sans-serif regular, about 3.15% of slide height, black #000000, except row 2 which is bold white #FFFFFF. Row number boxes and text bars are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows, no gradients. Row 1 and row 3 use light grey #D7D8D6 fill; row 2 uses red #DB0011 fill. Header rule is a black line about 0.3% thick; footer rule is a red #DB0011 line about 0.3% thick. Page number in Arial-style sans-serif about 1.7% of slide height, colour #252525.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the numbered priority tags and icons that tie slides 4, 12 and 13 to 27, the agenda slides that mark section changes, the action titles, and the red-for-HSBC highlighting against grey peers on slides 7, 10 and 21.

**Text**

Strategic priorities
Completing period of transformation; platform for growth
Transformation since 2011
Divested or exited 110 businesses / and geographies, reducing Group / footprint from 87 countries to 67
Reduced Risk Weighted Assets by / USD349bn or 29%1
Globalised the organisation / around 4 Global Businesses, / supported by Global Functions
Introduced robust financial crime / risk management capabilities
Invested USD7bn “Costs to Achieve” / to realise cost efficiencies of / USD6.1bn p.a. from our global / platform and built digital / capabilities
Shifted business to Asia and / faster-growing markets with Asia / representing c.50% of Group / revenue and c.75% of Group / profits2
Strengthened network to support / global trade and capital flows
Demonstrated ability to execute
Next phase of strategy: Return to growth and value creation
Growth
Deliver growth / from areas of / strength
1
Accelerate growth from our Asian franchise, and / Insurance and Asset Management in Asia
Be the leading bank to support drivers of global / investment: China-led Belt and Road Initiative / and the transition to a low carbon economy
2
Complete establishment of UK ring-fenced bank, / increase mortgage market share, grow commercial / customer base, and improve customer service
3
Gain market share and deliver growth from our / international network
Turnaround
Turnaround of / low-return / businesses
4
Turn around our US business
5
Improve capital efficiency; redeploy capital into / higher return businesses
6
Create capacity for increasing investments in / growth and technology through efficiency gains
Customer
Build a bank for / the future that / puts the / customer at the / centre
7
Enhance customer centricity and customer service / through investments in technology
Invest in digital capabilities to deliver improved / customer service
Expand the reach of HSBC, including partnerships
Safeguard our customers and deliver industry- / leading financial crime standards
Our People
Empower our / people
8
Simplify the organisation and invest in future / skills
1. Period 2015-17
2. Adjusted basis
12

**Layout**

Single slide with a bold red title across the top left below a thin black rule under a small italic kicker, and a thin red footer rule near the bottom. The content is two columns: a narrow grey left panel (about 5.6% to 35% of width, 14.7% to 90.7% of height) listing transformation bullets, and a right area with a red section header and eight numbered items grouped into four horizontal bands (Growth, Turnaround, Customer, Our People) separated by thin grey rules, with the category icon and label in the left of each band and red italic theme text beside it. Text is left aligned; footnotes sit bottom left and the page number bottom right at about 96% x.

**Visuals**

Four black monochrome icons sit in the right-hand band labels. (1) Growth: a square with light grey hatched background containing four ascending black bar-chart columns of increasing height and a black upward-pointing arrow running diagonally over them, box about 35.6% x 25.5% y, 6.8% by 9.7%. (2) Turnaround: a black circle at centre with a white dollar sign, surrounded by a thick black circular arrow loop with arrowheads at upper left and right, about 36.4% x 46.8% y, 5.6% by 8.1%. (3) Customer: a group of three black silhouetted adult figures, centre figure largest and in front, two smaller figures behind at left and right, about 36.1% x 64.3% y, 5.8% by 7.8%. (4) Our People: a single black silhouette of a person with both arms raised upward in a celebratory pose, about 37.6% x 81.7% y, 3.5% by 5.0%. A solid red right-pointing triangle sits at the right end of the left panel header at about 31.0% x 17.0% y, 2.9% by 2.0%. Eight solid red square number tiles (#DB0011) with white bold numerals 1 to 8 sit at the left of each item. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A bubble chart plots revenue against return on equity for each growth area, with bubble size showing targeted revenue growth, which makes the relative value of each opportunity easy to compare.
- Review: The weaknesses are density and some unclear charts: slides 13 and 29 carry visuals that do not communicate on their own (a bubble chart with no readable y or x scale values on slide 13, a waterfall with no values on most steps on slide 29), and many pages (20, 22, 25, 27) are tiny-text tables that a room audience could not read.
- Worth keeping: Keep the numbered priority tags and icons that tie slides 4, 12 and 13 to 27, the agenda slides that mark section changes, the action titles, and the red-for-HSBC highlighting against grey peers on slides 7, 10 and 21.
- Suggested repair: Label the waterfall steps on slide 29 with values, add readable axes or scale cues to the bubble chart on slide 13, and cut the footnote-sized text on slides 20, 22 and 25 so the headline numbers stand out.

**Layout**

Two-column layout below a full-width header: a left panel of about 30% width holding a bar chart and a right panel of about 58% width holding a bubble chart, both starting at about 22% from the top and ending near 85%. The title sits top-left at about 7% from the top, with a section tag '1-3' in a red box to its left; a small italic section label sits above a thin black rule at about 5.7%. Left-aligned text and panel headings share a left margin near 5.6%. A red rule at about 91.6% spans the width above a footnote at the bottom left; the page number is at bottom right near 95.4%.

**Visuals**

Left: vertical bar chart titled 'Revenue (reported), USDbn, 2011-2020'. Three grey bars with printed values: 2011 = 72.3, 2016 = 48.0, 2017 = 51.4; a fourth dashed-outline empty box labelled 2020 Target with no value. The axis breaks are marked with double slashes between 2011 and 2016 and between 2016 and 2017 and before 2020. No y-axis or scale is printed. Annotations: an ellipse labelled 'CAGR -8%' linked by a black elbow connector to the 2011 bar and by a long black arrow down to the 2016 bar; a small ellipse '+7%' on that connector with a black elbow arrow down to the 2017 bar; a large ellipse 'mid single digit growth, p.a.' with a thick black arrow pointing up-right from it toward the 2020 target box. A light grey (#DBDBDB) right-pointing chevron sits between the two panels. No legend. Right: bubble chart titled 'Targeting revenue opportunities in high growth areas with returns well above cost of equity'. Y-axis labelled 'Market Growth' with a black axis line and no numeric scale; X-axis labelled 'RoTE' (bottom right) with an axis break marked by double slashes near 79%; 'Cost of Equity' in red bold italic at the bottom left, marking a vertical dashed grey line at about 48.5%. A horizontal dashed line at about 67.3% marks the 5.8% (World Nominal GDP Growth) reference in red bold. Bubbles in red (#DB0011) with white labels outside: Low carbon economy/Sustainable Finance (small), Asia Wealth (medium), Asset Management (Asia) (small), Belt and Road (small), Insurance (Asia) with footnote 1 (medium), PRD (small white-outlined circle overlapping ASEAN), ASEAN (medium), Hong Kong (large, overlapping Transaction Banking), Transaction Banking/International network (large, outlined in white where it overlaps Hong Kong), UK Ring-fenced Bank (large). Size key at top right: a dashed-outline empty circle with text 'Size represents targeted revenue growth, 2017-2020; equal to c.USD1bn'. Top-right corner icon: a dark charcoal filled circle containing a white bar chart with an upward arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with a clean corporate look. Titles and accents in red #DB0011; body and chart text black #000000; page number dark grey #252525; bars mid grey #B4B4B4. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif). Main title is regular weight, about 4.0% of slide height (roughly 6.5% in cap-height terms per line). Panel headings are bold about 1.9%. Body and chart labels regular about 1.9% to 2.1%. Thin black rule under the section label; thin red rule under each panel heading; thin dashed black lines for reference lines; red rule above footer. No shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners except the ellipse annotations and circles.

**Layout**

Single slide with a red section number and title across the top left, a legend of three square groups and a dark round icon at the top right, and a two-column body: a horizontal bar chart with PBT ovals on the left (about 5.6% to 42% of width) and four stacked lettered opportunity panels on the right (about 43% to 94%). Left margin is about 5.6% and right margin about 5.5%; red rules close the header and the body at the bottom. Page number 14 sits bottom right at about 96% x and 95% y.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart titled Franchise in Asia (reported, ex BoCom), Revenue, 2017 USDbn, with seven grey bars (#B4B4B4) on a vertical black baseline, bars growing rightwards from the axis with value labels at bar ends: Hong Kong (RBWM and CMB) 11.4; Hong Kong (GB&M, GPB and Corporate Centre) 4.7; ASEAN 3.1; China 2.4 (with a red 0.2 label box marking the PRD portion); India 2.4; Australia 0.9; Other Asia 0.9. No axis scale or gridlines are printed. A red vertical marker line and red label PRD sit on the China bar. To the right, a column headed PBT, 2017 USDbn holds seven white ellipse outlines with bold italic values: 7.5, 2.1, 1.2, 0.5, 0.9, 0.4, 0.8 (Hong Kong RBWM and CMB, Hong Kong GB&M GPB and Corporate Centre, ASEAN, China, India, Australia, Other Asia). The right column Targeted revenue growth by 2020 (USD) uses square markers: A has three red squares, B has one red and two white, C has two red and one white, D has one red and two white. Legend top right: three red squares (>3bn), two red and one white (>1bn), one red and two white (>0.2bn). Icon top right: dark charcoal circle with a white rising bar chart and upward arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and section numbers in red #DB0011, Arial regular. Body text black #000000 in Arial regular. Section headers in Arial bold red #DB0011 with underlines in red. Lettered panels use bold black Arial headings on light grey #E0E1DF bars. **Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page with a red title at top left below a small italic section label and a thin black rule; the title sits in the top band with a dark icon at top right. The left column (about 5.6% to 50.9% of width) stacks a bar-chart block above a product-positions table; the right column is a black-outlined box (52.7% to 95% width, 16.2% to 90.7% height) containing three grey label blocks each beside bulleted text. Red section rules sit under each heading, a red footer rule spans the bottom above three footnotes at bottom left, and the page number 15 sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart (column chart, two bars) titled HSBC market share, %, with the row label Total banking assets in Hong Kong1, USDtn above it. Bars: 2005 red bar labelled 24%; 2017 (footnote 2) red bar labelled 25%, slightly taller than the 2005 bar. No y-axis numbers or range are printed; bar heights are about proportional to 24 and 25 with a baseline axis line under the bars. Above the bars, grey ovals show total banking assets: USD0.9tn (2005) and USD2.9tn (2017). A CAGR 2005-17 column on the right shows a grey-outlined oval with 10% for total assets and a red-outlined oval with 10% for HSBC market share, labelled Asset growth beneath. Product table with rows: Customer accounts #1 (red oval); Mortgages 37% and #1 (red ovals); Loans and advances to customers #1 (red oval); Credit cards >40% (red oval); DCM #1 (red oval); Trade finance #1 (red oval). Thin grey horizontal dividers separate the rows. Right panel: three grey (#B4B4B4) label blocks with black bold text; red square bullets and red en-dash sub-bullets. Top-right icon: dark charcoal (#333333) circle with a white ascending bar chart and upward arrow. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with flat, no-gradient styling. Titles and section headings in red #DB0011 (Arial Bold for section headings, Arial regular for main title). Body text black #000000. Accents red #DB0011 for rules, bars, ovals and bullets; grey #B4B4B4 for label blocks and divider lines. Typeface Arial (Helvetica-like sans-serif). Main title in regular weight at about 4.0% of slide height; section headings bold at about 2.1%; body text regular at about 2.1%; footnotes about 1.2%. Thin 0.2 pt black rule under the header line; red rules under headings; the outer frame of the right panel is a square-cornered black outline; ovals fully rounded; no shadows.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page: a left column (about x 5.5% to 51%) holding two bar charts and a bullet list, and a right column (about x 52% to 94.5%) enclosed in a thin black rectangular frame holding three grey label blocks with bullets. The title sits top-left below a small italic section label 'Strategic priorities' and a black rule, beside a red '1B' tag; the title is left-aligned at about x 9%, y 7%. A red rule closes the content at about y 91.6%, and the page number sits bottom-right at x 96%, y 95%. Left margin about 5.5%, right margin about 5.5%.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, Revenue, USDbn: vertical bar chart with four bars on a horizontal baseline at about y 39.2% of slide height (x 5.5% to 46.4%). Bars left to right: 2014 red #DB0011 value 0.18 (bar about 1.7% tall); 2017 red value 0.22 (about 2.0% tall); 2020 target grey #C0C0C0 labelled c.0.5 (about 4.6% tall); Medium-term target light grey #C0C0C0 labelled >USD1bn (about 9.1% tall). No y-axis, no gridlines, no legend. Chart 2, Loans & advances to customers, USDbn: same format, baseline at about y 63.3%. Bars: 2014 red labelled 4.1 (about 2.6% tall); 2017 red labelled 6.2 (about 3.9% tall); 2020 target grey labelled >10bn (about 6.2% tall); Medium-term target #B4B4B4 labelled >20bn (about 12.2% tall). Red bars are the actuals, grey bars are targets. Right panel: three grey #B4B4B4 rectangular label blocks at x 53.6% w 12.4%: Emerging Middle Class (y 24.5% to 45.9%), Industrial up-grade / cross-border connectivity (y 48.2% to 67.7%), New business capabilities (y 69.8% to 89.3%), separated by two thin grey divider lines (#A0A0A0, about 0.1% thick) at y 47.0% and y 68.6%, spanning x 66.8% to 94.2%. Icon top-right: dark circular badge with a white rising bar chart and upward arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with a clean corporate look. Titles and section headings are red #DB0011; body text and labels are black #000000; the page number is dark grey #252525. Accents are red #DB0011 (tag, rules, bullet squares, actual-value bars) and grey #B4B4B4 / #C0C0C0 (target bars, label blocks). Typeface is Arial (neo-grotesque sans-serif) throughout; the title is regular weight at about 4.0% of slide height, headings and bold labels are Arial Bold at about 2.1%, body text is Arial regular at about 2.1%, chart labels about 2.0%. Thin black rule under the header, red rule above the footer, thin black frame around the right panel with square corners, no shadows or gradients, no rounded corners.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a wide left panel (about 5.6% to 56.7% of width) holds a stacked column chart, and a narrower right panel (about 60.5% to 94.3%) holds three stacked bar-pair charts. The title sits top-left below the small italic section label 'Strategic priorities' with a full-width thin black rule between them, and the red 1C tag sits at the left of the title. Section headers are bold red with thin red rules beneath them. A source footnote block sits at bottom left above the page number '17' at bottom right; a red rule spans the width just above the footnotes. Left-aligned text with margins of about 5.6% on the left and about 5.7% on the right.

**Visuals**

Chart 1 (left): stacked column chart titled 'Private financial wealth1, USDtn' with a column per year for 2014, 2016 and 2021E, plus a 2016-21E CAGR column of bubbles at right. Totals above columns: 2014 152, 2016 166, 2021E 223. Segments from bottom to top: Asia ex Japan (red #DB0011) 21% in 2014, 23% in 2016, 28% in 2021E; Japan (mid grey #B4B4B4) 10%, 9%, 7%; W. Europe (light grey #D7D8D6) 25%, 24%, 22%; N. America (dark grey #4B4B4B) 34%, 33%, 33%; MENA, L. America and E. Europe (thin blue segments, from #C2CFE3 light blue to #4A6FA5 dark blue) are unlabelled at the top of each column. Series labels at left of the 2014 column: E. Europe, L. America, MENA, N. America, W. Europe, Japan, Asia ex Japan, with thin leader lines to the top three segments. CAGR bubbles to the right of the 2021E column: N. America 5.6% (dark grey oval), W. Europe 3.5% (light grey oval), Japan 1.7% (mid grey oval), Asia ex Japan 9.9% (red oval, bold italic, white text), each with a thin grey leader line to its segment. No value axis; no legend (labels act as legend). Highlighted: the red Asia ex Japan series and its 9.9% CAGR bubble.
Chart 2 (top right): 'Middle class in Asia2', unit '# of people, bn'. Grey bars (#B4B4B4) with value labels: 2015 1.4 and 2030E 3.5, on a black baseline. Oval callout '2.5x' with black arrow pointing up-right between the bars.
Chart 3 (middle right): 'Average household annual income3', unit 'USD‘000'. Grey bars: 2017 14.5 and 2030E 32.6. Oval callout '2.3x' with black arrow.
Chart 4 (bottom right): 'HNWI financial wealth4', unit 'USDtrn'. Grey bars: 2017 20.8 and 2025E 42.1. Oval callout '2.0x' with black arrow.
No axis numbering or ranges are printed on any chart. Icon: top-right circular logo, dark charcoal circle (#333333) with a white rising bar chart and upward arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles are red #DB0011 in regular-weight sans-serif (Arial-like, Helvetica style), main title about 4.0% of slide height. Section headers are bold red #DB0011 Arial at 2.1% of slide height, with red rules (#DB0011, thin, about 0.2% line weight) beneath them. Body and labels are black #000000 Arial, about 2.1% of slide height. Subtitles are red #DB0011 Arial regular. Italic section label 'Strategic priorities' in black Arial italic, 2.1%. Top black rule about 0.1% weight. Data colours: red #DB0011, mid grey #B4B4B4, light grey #D7D8D6, dark grey #4B4B4B, blues #4A6FA5 / #9DB0D0 / #C2CFE3. Bubble labels are italic, except 9.9% bold italic. Footnotes in Arial at 1.2% of slide height, black. Page number Arial in #252525 at 1.7%. No gradients, shadows or rounded corners on bars; CAGR bubbles and callouts are fully rounded ovals.

**Review notes**

- Review: Consistent chart styling, red-for-HSBC colour logic and action-style titles make most pages readable as claim plus evidence, and the pairing of data with short claims on slides 7, 8 and 18 works well.

**Layout**

Single slide with a red title rule-free header: small italic section label 'Strategic priorities' top-left above a thin black rule spanning the width, then a large red left-aligned title with a red 1C tag box at its left, and a legend of square markers top-right. Three columns sit below the title: a left waterfall chart (about x 5.6-30 %), a central opportunities list (x 35.3-94.4 %) with grey full-width category bands, and a narrow right column (x 82-95 %) for targeted growth squares; a red rule closes the content near y 93 %, with a footnote at bottom-left (x 5.6 %, y 93.4 %) and page number '18' bottom-right (x 96.2 %, y 95.4 %).

**Visuals**

Floating-bar (waterfall-style) chart, horizontal bars stacked vertically, titled 'Wealth in Asia already a USD5.1bn business for HSBC today' with subtitle 'Revenue (reported) in USDbn, Asia, 2017'. Vertical black axis line at about x 16.4 % running from y 27 % to y 92 %; no numeric axis scale or ticks. Five categories top to bottom with printed values: Wealth in Asia 5.1 (solid red #DB0011 bar from the axis, about 14.2 % of slide width, total bar, highlighted); Private Bank 0.7 (grey #B4B4B4 floating bar, about x 28.7-30.6 %, label 0.7 inside bar); RBWM Wealth 2.4 (grey floating bar, about x 22.1-28.7 %, label inside); Insurance 1.8 (grey floating bar, label inside, about x 17.2-22.1 %); Asset Management 0.3 (thin grey bar at the axis, label 0.3 beside it). Thin grey dotted connector lines link the bar ends between rows. No legend for the chart itself. Top-right legend of three small square groups with black outline: '>USD1bn' with three red filled squares, '>0.4bn' with two red and one white square, '>0.1bn' with one red and two white squares. Right column 'Targeted revenue growth by 2020' with USD heading and square markers per row: Wealth management row red, red, white; Insurance row red, red, white; Asset management row red, white, white. Bulleted red square markers (Wingdings) for each opportunity item. Top-right circular icon: dark charcoal #2B2B2B filled circle with a white bar-chart-with-upward-arrow glyph, about x 94.2-99.4 %, y 1.5-8.9 %.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White #FFFFFF background, clean corporate look. Title text red #DB0011 in Arial regular, about 4.0 % of slide height. Section label 'Strategic priorities' black #000000 Arial italic about 2.1 %. Column headers red #DB0011 Arial bold about 2.1 %. Body text black #000000 Arial regular about 2.1 %; category labels black Arial bold about 2.1 %. Accents red #DB0011 for the 1C tag (white bold Arial text on red square), bullet markers, the header rules and the bottom rule. Thin red horizontal rules (about 0.2 % height) under the column headers and near y 93 %; a thin black rule under the section label at y 4 %. Grey category bands #E0E1DF, full height about 3.4 % of slide, square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Bars #B4B4B4 grey and #DB0011 red, flat fills, no outlines. Legend and targeted squares are small square boxes with black thin outlines and red or white fill.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Strategic priorities / UK presents an opportunity for growth after successful / completion of ring-fencing / >USD1bn / >0.4bn / >0.1bn / Targeted / revenue / growth by / 2020 / Ring-Fenced Bank setup / close to completion / Opportunities and areas of investment / USD / HSBC is positioned for sustainable growth; 14% deposit market share1 and a 7% mortgage market share2 / UK ring-fencing remains on track ahead of the July 2018 legal separation - six months ahead of the regulatory deadline: / On the 21 May 2018, the High Court approved the UK Ring-Fenced Transfer Scheme / More than 95% of technical and IT related transfers have already been successfully completed / Retail banking / Target mortgage growth (high single digit) by embedding controlled intermediary channel expansion3 / Enhancing the multi-brand strategy to drive growth and acquire new customers / Corporate banking / Leverage our unique global access to support commercial customers' trade and overseas banking needs / Improve penetration of mid market segment through additional on-boarding capacity and renewed focus on 'fast growth cities or sectors' / Improve CRM and data analytics to drive better value segmentation of clients (in particular Business Banking) / Digital and customer satisfaction / Target a consistent top 3 position in the UK for customer satisfaction, via journey improvements, digital investment and simplification / Capitalise on AI, Data analytics and Open Banking to develop immersive customer experiences (e.g., Connected Money app) / Grow collaboration opportunities across business lines and brands / 1. Source: CACI Retail Finance Benchmark, 2017 / 2. Source: Council of Mortgage Lenders (CML), 2017 / 3. No change in risk appetite / 19

**Layout**

White slide with a single content area: a red numbered section tag and a two-line red title across the top, a legend of square markers at top right, and a three-column grid below: left column of bullets (about 5% to 37% width), a centre column of three grey row boxes with icons and bullets (about 32% to 95%), and a narrow right column for the revenue targets (about 88% to 95%). Titles sit top-left with red rules beneath section headings; a full-width red rule sits above three numbered footnotes at bottom left and the page number 19 sits bottom right. Left margin about 5.6%, right margin about 5.5%.

**Visuals**

Three grey row boxes (#D7D8D6) in the centre column each hold a black circular-outline icon and a bold label: Retail banking (icon of a hand holding a banknote with a dollar sign), Corporate banking (icon of a building beside a standing businessman with briefcase), Digital and customer satisfaction (icon of a thumbs-up). Right of the centre column, a phone illustration (white handset with rounded corners, red bank logo tile in the centre with the tagline 'Your banks. Your accounts. Together in one place.' and four small coloured app icons above: a blue money bag, a green piggy bank, a red wallet and a purple building) overlaps the lower right of the centre area. Red square markers show the targeted revenue growth: next to Retail banking, 2 filled red squares and 1 empty square; next to Corporate banking, 2 filled red squares and 1 empty square. Legend at top right: 3 filled red squares = >USD1bn; 2 filled and 1 empty = >0.4bn; 1 filled and 2 empty = >0.1bn. Top-right corner shows a dark grey circular logo with white rising bar-chart and arrow icon.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Titles and section headings red #DB0011 in Arial Bold (headings) and Arial regular (main title). Body text black #000000 in Arial regular with bold emphasis phrases in Arial Bold. Accents red #DB0011 for bullets, rules, section number square and markers. Main title about 4.0% of slide height, body about 2.1%, footnotes about 1.2%, section tag about 2.1% italic. Line and shape styles: thin red horizontal rules about 0.15% of height; thin grey separators (#BFBFBF) between rows; grey row boxes have no corners rounded, no shadows; legend squares are small outlined squares with red fill or white fill and black outline. No gradients or shadows.

**Layout**

Two-column body under a two-line red title, with a small section label above the title and a corner icon at top right. The left column 'Investments are delivering' is a table of metrics with year headers 2015 and 2017 and a tick or arrow column; the right column 'Opportunities and areas of investment' is four grey-banded blocks of bullets with three square markers each, under a 'Targeted revenue growth by 2020' heading. Both columns sit inside thin red-bordered boxes that begin under the headings. A thin red rule runs across the bottom above four numbered footnotes in two columns, and the page number sits bottom right. Left margin is about 5.6% and the title starts at about 9.1%.

**Visuals**

Table on the left with rows grouped by category: Global Trade and Receivables Finance (Trade Finance rank 1: 2015 #1, 2017 #1, green tick; Hong Kong market share 2: 10.8% to 13.8%, green up arrow); Global Liquidity and Cash Management (Average GLCM balances: c$470bn to c$530bn, green up arrow; Hong Kong market share 3: 22.8% to 26.3%, green up arrow); FX (FX corporates rank 4: #1 to #1, green tick; FX institutional rank 4: #7 to #3, green up arrow); Securities Services (Assets under custody: $6.2trn to $7.7trn, green up arrow; Market share 5: 5.4% to 5.8%, green up arrow; Asia rank 6: #1 to #1, green tick). Values sit in white outlined ellipses with thin black outlines. Green icons are #00B050 Wingdings ticks and block arrows. Legend at top right has three rows of three small squares: filled red squares for >USD1bn (three filled), two filled and one white for >0.4bn, one filled and two white for >0.1bn. Right column squares per block in red and white: Digitise and grow Trade Finance three filled? shows two red and one white; Strengthen global leadership shows three red; FX business growth shows one red and two white; Grow Securities Services business shows two red and one white. Top-right corner icon: a dark charcoal circle (about #2B2B2B) with white bar chart and rising arrow. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white 16:9 slide with a thin black rule under the section label at the top and a red rule across the bottom above the footnotes. The section label sits top left with the title below it, a numbered red square marker at the left of the title, and a circular dollar icon at top right. The left column (about 5.6% to 50% width) holds a bar chart with a heading and subheading; a vertical red divider at about 45.5% width separates it from the right column (about 46% to 94%), which is a three-row stacked panel of grey label boxes on the left with a pie chart and two bar charts on the right. Footnotes are at the bottom left in small type; the page number 21 sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three charts and one icon. (1) Left: vertical bar chart titled Cross-border GB&M and CMB client revenue, 2017, USDbn, with a callout 'Client revenue from US-managed companies booked outside US' pointing to the US bar. Y-axis labelled 0, 1, 2 (USDbn, range 0 to about 2.5, no unit label printed on axis). Five categories: US, UK, China, France, Hong Kong. No values printed on the bars; estimates: US about 2.4, UK about 1.2, China about 0.9, France about 0.5, Hong Kong about 0.5. Bars are grey #B4B4B4 except the US bar, which is red #DB0011 and highlighted. Beneath the axis a caption reads '% of Group client outbound revenue' with pill labels 24% (red pill, white bold text, highlighted), 13%, 9%, 6%, 6% (light grey pills #D9D9D9, black text) under US, UK, China, France, Hong Kong. (2) Top right: pie chart with two slices, USD 68% in red #DB0011 with white label, and Other 32% in grey #B4B4B4 with black label; a thin white separator at the 12 o'clock edge; labels 'USD' and 'Other' sit outside the slices. Pie title is the grey box text 'USD represents 68% of payments volume for HSBC'. (3) Middle right: vertical bar chart with values 17.1% JP Morgan, 16.2% Citigroup, 10.4% Bank of America, 9.4% HSBC (red #DB0011, bold label, bold category name), 9.2% BoNY Mellon; all bars grey #B4B4B4 except HSBC; baseline is a thin dark grey line; no y-axis. (4) Bottom right: vertical bar chart with bars Overall c.19% (dark grey #4A4A4A, bold label), Hong Kong 29%, Germany 16%, UK 16%; bars grey #B4B4B4 except Overall; baseline thin dark grey line; no y-axis. (5) Top right corner: circular black icon with a white dollar sign inside and two curved black arrows forming a cycle around it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style: white background #FFFFFF, titles and section headings and the main slide headline in red #DB0011, body and chart text black #000000, grey label boxes #B4B4B4 with bold black text, accents red #DB0011 and dark grey #4A4A4A for the Overall bar. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like sans-serif), regular for the headline and body, bold for section heads and emphasis. Headline size about 4.0% of slide height (cap height about 2.8%); body text about 2.1% of slide height; footnotes about 1.2%; section label about 2.1% italic; page number about 1.7%. Rules are thin: a black line under the section label, red horizontal rules under the left heading and the bottom footnote rule, and a red vertical divider about 0.2% wide. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners on bars; the pills are fully rounded ellipses.

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Label the waterfall steps on slide 29 with values, add readable axes or scale cues to the bubble chart on slide 13, and cut the footnote-sized text on slides 20, 22 and 25 so the headline numbers stand out.

Strategic priorities

Completed run-off of the CML legacy portfolio; reduced receivables from USD24bn at end-2014 to USD0bn at end 2017
Improved RBWM PBT, revenue and deposits; migration of >1mn customers to new core banking platform; launched CMB returns improvement and infrastructure rebuild
Achieved non-objection to US capital plan as part of CCAR in 2016 and 2017; first return of capital to the Group (USD5.4bn) since 2006
International client revenue booked in the US up c.10% YoY; US client revenue booked outside of the US (outbound) is up c.15% YoY
Improved profitability driven by global business organic growth in:
CMB: Targeting greater share in corporates, particularly international mid market companies and subsidiaries, through increased coverage and sector focus; supported by selected cash management and lending product expansion
RBWM: Targeted growth within international segment and higher-return products and business banking
GBM: Sector coverage and greater share of foreign multi-national clients in the US
Further efficiency gains to help fund reinvestments; invest in innovation leveraging Group technology solutions
Return to regular dividend payments to Group
1. US geographic basis
2. HSBC North America Holdings (‘HNAH’) legal entity basis. Reported RoTE for 2017 was -4.3% and included a 5.2% adverse impact from the one time write down of deferred tax assets due to US Tax Reform
3. Principal Business RoTE for 2017 excluding the one time write down of deferred tax assets due to US Tax Reform, CML and deferred tax assets disallowed for capital purposes would be 2.2%
4. Revenue from international clients is derived from an allocation of Adjusted revenue based on internal management information. International clients are businesses and individuals with an international presence; YoY growth refers to 2017
22

**Layout**

Single slide on a white field with a header row at the top: italic 'Strategic priorities' at the top-left above a thin black rule that runs the full content width. The red numbered title sits below, starting at about 9% of the width with the red '4' square to its left, and wraps to two lines. Below the title, two columns of equal width (left from about 5.6% to 46%, right from about 48% to 95%) each hold a red bold subheading with a thin red underline, a chart, and a bordered bullet box. A red rule spans the width above the footnotes, which sit at the bottom-left with the page number at bottom-right. Margins are about 5.6% left and about 5% right.

**Visuals**

Left chart: stacked column chart titled 'Adjusted PBT, USDm' (units USDm, no printed axis scale or gridlines), with a baseline at about 46% of slide height. Four columns for 2014, 2015, 2016 and 2017. Series 'US Principal' is a solid grey (#B3B3B3) bottom segment and series 'CML' is an unfilled white segment with a black outline on top; the row labels 'CML' and 'US Principal' sit at the left of the first column. Printed values: 2014 total 1,201 with US Principal 464 (CML segment about 737, implied); 2015 total 974 with US Principal 494 (CML about 480, estimate); 2016 total 556 with US Principal 387 (CML about 169, estimate); 2017 total 737 with the segment labelled 920 inside a pink (#FF9999) column outlined in red (#DB0011) that extends below the baseline as a pale outline. The 2017 column is the highlighted point. Year labels 2014, 2015, 2016, 2017 sit below the baseline. Right chart: waterfall titled 'RoTE' with superscript 2 and no axis or scale. Five steps left to right: 2017 bar 0.9% (superscripts 2,3) in pale red #FF9999, short; 'Capital reductions completed & Tax reform' grey gradient step (about 3 units, estimate); 'PBT growth' grey gradient step (larger, about 2.5 times the previous step, estimate); 'Future capital actions' grey gradient step (smaller, estimate); 2020 bar '> 6%' (superscript 2) solid red #DB0011 reaching the top of the chart. Dashed grey connector lines link the top of each step to the next. Baseline is a thin black line. Category labels 2017, Capital reductions completed & Tax reform, PBT growth, Future capital actions, 2020 are below the baseline. Icon: a black filled circle with a white dollar sign '$' encircled by two black curved arrows with arrowheads (a currency/cycle icon) at top-right. Two bordered boxes contain bullets and no images.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

23

**Layout**

White slide with a red numbered title block across the top: a small red square with white number 5 sits at the left of a two-line red title, with a thin black rule above the title spanning nearly the full width under the 'Strategic priorities' kicker. Below, the content is split into two columns: a left chart column (x 5.6% to 73%) under a red bold heading 'RWA mix by Global Business' with a red underline, and a right column (x 64.3% to 94%) under a red bold heading 'Initiatives' with a red underline. The left column's chart axis runs at y about 79% with year labels beneath; the 'Rev as % of RWA' row sits at the bottom left. A thin red rule at y about 91.5% spans the width above a one-line footnote at y about 93.4%. Slide number 23 sits bottom right at about x 96%, y 95%. Left margin is about 5.6% and right margin about 4%.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked and waterfall-style bar chart titled 'RWA mix by Global Business', subtitle 'Group RWAs, USDbn'. Bars from left to right: 2014 single solid navy bar labelled 1,220 with no segment split; a bracket above it labelled -29% (ellipse outline) with an elbow arrow pointing down to the 2017 bar; 2017 stacked bar totalling 871, segments from top: GB&M 34% (light grey #D6D7D9), CMB 35% (mid grey #767576), RBWM 14% (dark grey), GPB 2% (thin white-outlined sliver), Corp Centre 15% (dark grey, about #3F3F3F), with segment labels to the left (GB&M, CMB, RBWM, GPB, Corp Centre); a floating red gradient bar (red #DB0011 fading to white at its bottom) labelled 'RWA saves' with dashed connector lines; a floating blue-to-white gradient bar labelled 'Business growth' with a dark grey dot and leader line down to the caption 'To support mid-single digit revenue growth', plus an ellipse above reading 'c.1-2% p.a.' with an elbow arrow down to 2020; 2020 target stacked bar: c.30% light grey (#D6D7D9), c.35% mid grey (#767576), 15-20% dark grey, and an unlabelled dark grey base segment (about #3F3F3F). No y-axis or numeric scale; values are as printed. Row under chart 'Rev as % of RWA1' with grey pill ellipses (about #D6D7D9, light fill): 5.0% under 2014, 5.9% under 2017, c.7% under 2020. Icon: black filled circle with white dollar sign and two black circular arrows around it, top right. Rule: thin black line under the kicker; red rules under section headings and above footnote. Initiatives panel: three stacked grey rectangles (about #B4B4B4) with bold black labels, each with red square bullets listing items to the right: RoTE implementation (Embed RoTE in Global Businesses and operating entities; Link incentives to value creation), Distribution (Develop distribution channel and increase distribution for wholesale lending; Free up capital/ balance sheet capacity and deploy to higher return business/clients), RWA optimisation (Operating entity RWA optimisation; Improve global booking model). Thin grey separator lines between the bullet groups.

**Design**

Background pure white #FFFFFF. Titles and accents red #DB0011; body text black #000000; kicker and slide number dark grey #252525. Typeface Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif), title regular weight at about 4.0% of slide height, section headings Arial Bold at about 2.1%, body Arial regular about 2.1%, chart axis labels Arial about 1.9%, footnote Arial about 1.2%. Flat shapes with no rounded corners and no shadows. Bars solid fills; the red and blue bars are vertical gradients. Pill ellipses light grey with no visible outline. Section rule lines red and about 0.2% of slide height thick.

### Slide 24

**Text**

Strategic priorities / 6 Maintain strong cost discipline, deliver positive jaws and create / capacity for increased investment / Create investment capacity and deliver overall positive adjusted jaws on a full year basis / Adjusted basis, USDbn / Revenue / Mid single digit / growth, p.a. / Total / operating / expenses / Low to mid single / digit growth, p.a. / 31.1 / c.4 / 4.5-5 / 5.5-6 / 6-6.5 / Investments / Investments of USD15-17bn (2018-2020) / Ability to invest is a prerequisite for the Group’s long-term / competitiveness / Investments aligned to strategic priorities / Managed through a strong approval and prioritisation / framework to deliver payback in the near to medium term / Ability to respond to changes in economic environment and / revenue development / No CTA2 in strategic plan; all investments to be made from / within the cost base of the Group / Costs (ex / investments) / Strong cost discipline and control to create investment / capacity / Implement strong cost discipline and control / Continue to benchmark our costs with the market / Absorb inflation through productivity gains / Maintain focus on improving business productivity / Maintain positive (adjusted) jaws on an annual basis each / year 2018-2020 / Jaws1 / positive / positive / positive / positive / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 1. Adjusted, on an annual basis / 2. Costs to Achieve / 24

**Layout**

Two-part slide on a white page: a small italic section label and thin black rule sit at the top left, with a red title beside a red box containing the number 6 across the upper part. Below, a left column holds a bar-style chart with row labels down its left edge, and a right column holds two text blocks; the lower band of the chart and the cost text sit on a light grey panel that spans the left and right columns beneath a dashed divider. A red rule runs above the footnotes at the bottom left, and the page number sits bottom right; the icon sits in the top right corner, and margins are about 6% at left and right.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart (columns) titled Adjusted basis, USDbn, with four columns for 2017, 2018, 2019 and 2020 on a shared baseline. Each column is split into an upper Investments segment (light grey, about #B3B3B3, with a grey gradient blending into the cost segment at the dashed divider) and a lower Costs (ex investments) segment in dark grey #767576 with a white diagonal axis-break mark across it. Labelled investment values: 2017 c.4 (top label 31.1 in bold above the column), 2018 4.5-5, 2019 5.5-6, 2020 6-6.5; the value axis has no printed scale or range. No legend. Two annotation ovals with arrows: a white oval reading Mid single digit growth, p.a. sits over a thin black arrow running from left to upper right above the columns, and a white oval reading Low to mid single digit growth, p.a. sits over a thin black elbow connector that drops from the 2017 column and ends in a downward arrow above the 2020 column. Below the baseline, four light grey pill-shaped ovals (about #D9D9D9, thin grey outline) each contain the italic word positive, one under each year, forming the Jaws row. Top-right icon: a solid black group-of-people icon (three figures, the centre one larger) on a transparent background.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the full slide, with a light grey #F3F3F3 panel over the lower half of the content area. White background #FFFFFF with a light grey panel #F3F3F3 behind the lower chart and cost text. Section label and body text are black #000000; the title, subtitle, unit line, the dashed-free red rule and the number box use red #DB0011; the bullet markers are red #DB0011. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like sans-serif), title regular weight, subtitles and headings bold, annotations italic. Title text is about 4.0% of slide height (cap height about 2.8%); body text about 2.1%; footnotes about 1.2%. Rules are thin black (section rule) and red (subtitle underline and footer rule). Dashed divider is thin grey #808080 with short dashes. No corner radii except the pill ovals, which are fully rounded; no shadows; gradient only on the investment segments.
